1. No cleaning-up process is applied.

tatrācāryā ādito vaiśvadevamantrānadhīyate | tatra coktaṃ ubhayataḥ pariṣecanaṃ - yathā purastāditi | tatpariṣecanamāntrāḥ pūrvaṃ vyākhyeyāḥ | aditirnāma devamātā | he adite anumanyasva | mayā kriyamāṇaṃ karma anujānīhi | anumatiḥ anumantrī ūnacandrā pūrṇamāsī | he anumate anumanyasva | sarasvatī vāgdevatā, tasyāssambuddhiḥ | he sarasvate anumanyasva | sarasvate iti chāndaso guṇaḥ | he deva savitaḥ | sarvasyānujñātā | asmānapi prasuva anujānīhi || om a̱gnaye̱ svāhā̍| somā̍y㌚ svāhā̍| viśveホhyo de̱vebhy㌚s svāhā̍| dhru̱vāyペ bhū̱māyペ svāhā̍| dhru̱v㌚kṣitペye̱ svāhā̍| a㌜yu̱t㌚kṣitペye̱ svāhā̍| a̱gnaye̍ sviṣṭ㌚kṛte̍ svāhā̍| adi̱te'nvペmam̐sthāḥ| anu̍m㌚te'nvペmam̐sthāḥ| sarペs-v㌚te'nvペmam̐sthāḥ| devペ savitaḥ̱ prāsā̍vīḥ|

atha vaiśvadevamantrāḥ | teṣāṃ mantrāṇāmupayoge dvādaśāhamadhaśśayyā | ityādi | upayogaḥ niyamapūrvakaṃ vidyāgrahaṇam | kartureva vratam | patnyā api kecidicchanti | om agnaye svāhā | yathā om- ityanujñākṣaraṃ, tathā svāhākāro haviḥpradānārthaḥ | somāya svāheti | imaṃ mantraṃ kecinnādhīyate | viśvebhyo devebhyaḥ | sarvebhyo devebhyaḥ | dhruvāya ekarūpāya vṛddhikṣayarahitāya | bhūmāya bhūmne brahmaṇe | bhūmā tveva vijijñāsitavyaḥ iti chāndogyam | dhruvā niścalā kṣitiḥ gatiḥ vyāptiryasya kāryavargaṃ prati, tasmai dhruvakṣitaye brahmaṇe | evaṃ acyutakṣitaye | evamagnaye sviṣṭakṛte | rudrognissviṣṭakṛt | sviṣṭaṃ suhutaṃ karotīti sviṣṭakṛt tasmai || pūrvavatpariṣecanam | anvamaṃsthāḥ | anujñātavatī | prāsāvīḥ anujñātavān || prathamaprapāṭhake prathamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ.

"sabhadhvarāyeti | tasyaiva bhagasya prasādena uṣasopi adhva rāya asmai sapatnāvādhanarūpāya yajñāya samanamanta, loḍathe laḍ, saṃnamantāṃ bhajantāmityarthaḥ | dadhikrovava dadhikrāvā anni: āpa ityanye | sa yathā śucaye padāya śuddhāya sthānāya kalpata tadvat | kiñca-vasuvidaṃ vasuneo lambhayitāraṃ bhagaṃ naḥ asmā nprati arvācīnaṃ aṃbhimukhaṃ āvahantu ke ? vājinaḥ vegavanta aśvā rathamiva yathā rathaṃ aśvā vahanti tadvat || 'uttarā ṛk vaiśvadevī-aśvāvatīriti| uṣāsaḥ uṣasaḥ aśva vatyaḥ geomatyaḥ vīravatyaśva bhūtvā ucchantu vyucchantu vyuṣṭā bhavantu sadaṃ sadā nityamityarthaḥ | ghṛtaṃ, upalakṣaṇametat, "kṣīraṃ sarpiḥ madhūdakam' ityādi duhānāḥ viśvataḥ sarvataḥ prapīnāḥ pravṛddhāḥ ye āsminsūkta 68 savyākhyānaikānnikāṇḍe, svatibhistadā naḥ || 15 || bhaveha catvāri ca || 15 || imāṃ khannābhyoṣaṃdhoṃ vīrudhaṃ balaṃvattamām | yayām̐ sapatroṃ bādhate yayāṃ saivindate patibh | uttānapaṇe surbhage saham̐māne sarhasvati | sapatriṃ me parāṃdhama nirdiṣṭāḥ agrajyādayaḥ te yūyaṃ naḥ asmān svastibhiḥ sadā pāta rakṣata || iti śrīharadattaviracite ekāgkiāṇḍamantravyākhyāne pañcadaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ, patyurvaśīkaraṇe pāṭhāyā utpāṭana *mantraḥ-imāṃ khanāmāṃta || imāṃ pāṭhākhyāṃ oṣadhīṃ khanāmi utpāṭyāmi | vīrudhaṃ vividhaṃ rohantīṃ pratānavatīmityarthaḥ | balavattamāṃ atiśayena vīryavatī mityarthaḥ | yayā oṣadhyā sapatrīṃ bādhate sarva eva strījanaḥ yayā ca samyak , vindate asādhāraṇaṃ labhate patim ||

77 prātarannei caturdomāṃ khannābhyudsau sūryo dvādaśādvā

dṛśāni rūpāṇi, tāni ca brahma brāhmaṇaḥ śaṃsati tasya ka thamanarthaśaṅketi | utaśabdaḥ samyagarthe, anekārthatvānnipātānām | evametadvadhūvāsasaḥ paridānaṃ catuthryanta kartavyaṃ, tathā ca vivāha nte āśvalāyanaḥ " sūryāvide vadhūvastraṃ dadyāt' ?iti | anye tu yakṣmagṛhītāyāḥ vāsaḥ etadvaiṣajyavid deyamupadiśyate ityāhuḥ | sūyāśabdenāpi ta eva mantrā ucyante | iti śrīharadattamiśraviracite ekādhikāṇḍamantravyākhyāne 78 dvitīyaśprārambhaḥ. uṣṇena vāyabudakenehyaditiḥ keśān vapatu|'ā paṃ undantu jīvaseṃ dīdhayutvāya varcase | jyokca sū varuṇasya vidvān | tenaṃ brahmāṇo vapatedamasyāyu 'athopanayanamantrāḥ | tatra uṣṇā apaḥ śītāsvānayatei uṣṇeneti | he vāyo! suprajāḥ prajayām̐ bhūyāsa suvīroṃ vīraissuvarcā va caisā supoṣaḥ poṣaissugṛhīṃ gṛhaissupatiḥ patyāṃ su medhā medhayāṃ subrahmā brahmacāribhiḥ | agrayeṃ sami dhamāharṣaṃ bṛhate jātavedase yathā tvarmame samidha samidhyastaṃ evaṃ māmāryuṣā varcasā sanyā medhayām̐ prajayā paśubhitraihmavarcasenānnārdena samedhaya svāhām̐ edhe'syedhiīmahi svāhā | 'samisi samedhiṣī ca prārthitena | dhuprajā ityādi | gatam * | atra bhūyāsamiti viśeṣaḥ | | patiḥ pālayitā ācāryaḥ | medhayā 0dhāraṇāvatyā dhiyā sumedhāḥ | subrahmā śeobhanaveda brahmacāribhiḥ sahādhyāpi bhiḥ 'pādaṃ sabrahmacāribhyaḥ' iti vacanāt ||

samidādhānamantrā- agraya iti || tubhyaṃ samidhaṃ imāṃ
